Q2. Fill in the blank: Priests were not to make themselves unclean for the dead, except for close family such as a mother, father, son, daughter, brother or unmarried ______. (21:1-3) Easy
Answer: sister. Priests had special holiness, yet family love still mattered.


Q3. Multiple choice: What service was restricted for a descendant of Aaron with one of the listed physical conditions? (21:17-23) Easy
A) Presenting offerings at the altar   B) Belonging to Aaron's family   C) Eating all holy food   D) Living among Israel
Answer: A. Presenting offerings at the altar. The restriction concerns altar service; verse 22 permits him to eat holy food. It is not a statement that disability removes a person's human dignity.


Q4. True or false: A priest with a blemish could not come near the altar to offer food to God, but could still eat the holy food. (21:21-22) Medium
Answer: True. A blemish barred the priest from offering, but not from holy food.


Q5. True or false: The priests were not to shave their heads or cut the corners of their beards. (21:5) Easy
Answer: True. Holiness set the priests apart in visible ways.


Q6. Fill in the blank: The LORD said, "I am the LORD who ______ you." (21:8) Medium
Answer: sanctify. God sets His people apart for Himself.


Q7. Multiple choice: Whom was the high priest instructed to marry? (21:10, 13-14) Medium
A) A foreign slave   B) A king's widow   C) Any woman without restriction   D) A virgin from his own people
Answer: D. A virgin from his own people. These verses concern the high priest specifically. The requirements for other priests are given separately in verses 7-9.


Q8. Multiple choice: Why did God require the priests to be holy? (21:6-8) Hard
A) Priests were wealthy   B) Priests ruled the people   C) Priests were chosen for their beauty   D) The offerings are God's food and He makes them holy
Answer: D. The offerings are God's food and He makes them holy. God's holy work requires holy workers.


Leviticus 22
Q1. Multiple choice: What was the age requirement for an animal offered as a sacrifice? (22:27) Medium
A) Born in the wilderness   B) Ten years old   C) Eight days old or more   D) Blind
Answer: C. Eight days old or more. Offerings were ordered with care.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Priests who were unclean were not to eat the holy things until ______. (22:6-7) Easy
Answer: evening. Uncleanness required washing and waiting.


Q3. Multiple choice: What had to happen to a thank offering? (22:29-30) Hard
A) It was eaten the same day, with none left until morning   B) It was sold   C) It was burned in the wilderness   D) Nothing was done with it
Answer: A. It was eaten the same day, with none left until morning. Thanksgiving was a joyful meal eaten before God.


Q4. Fill in the blank: You shall not offer to the LORD an animal that is blind, injured, or ______. (22:22) Medium
Answer: maimed. God asks for the best from His people.


Q5. Multiple choice: Who, besides a priest, could eat the sacred food under these rules? (22:10-13) Easy
A) Every hired worker   B) Eligible members of his household, including those born there or bought   C) Every foreign guest   D) Every visitor
Answer: B. Eligible members of his household, including those born there or bought. The text distinguishes eligible household members from visitors and hired workers and gives conditions for a priest's daughter.


Q6. True or false: Animals with a blemish could not be offered to the LORD. (22:20) Easy
Answer: True. Offerings were to be whole and without defect.


Q7. True or false: God said, "I am the LORD who sanctifies you, who brought you out of the land of Egypt to be your God." (22:32-33) Hard
Answer: True. God's holiness is tied to His act of deliverance.


Q8. True or false: A thank offering was to be offered so that it would be accepted. (22:29) Medium
Answer: True. Thanksgiving was to be offered with a willing heart.


Leviticus 23
Q1. Fill in the blank: The seventh day is a Sabbath of solemn rest, a holy ______. (23:3) Easy
Answer: convocation. God's people gather before Him for rest and worship.


Q2. True or false: Leviticus 23 said that the Day of Atonement was to be a day of self-denial. (23:27) Medium
Answer: True. The day called for humility and rest before God.


Q3. Multiple choice: Why were the Israelites to live in booths for seven days? (23:42-43) Hard
A) To hide from enemies   B) To punish the poor   C) To remember that God made them dwell in booths when He brought them out of Egypt   D) To sell goods
Answer: C. To remember that God made them dwell in booths when He brought them out of Egypt. Israel's temporary dwelling was a reminder of God's provision.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which feast was held on the fourteenth day of the first month? (23:5) Easy
A) Booths   B) Trumpets   C) Weeks   D) Passover
Answer: D. Passover. Passover marked God's deliverance from Egypt.


Q5. Multiple choice: What did the priest wave before the LORD at the Feast of Firstfruits? (23:10-11) Medium
A) A golden lamp   B) A trumpet   C) A bundle of ash   D) A sheaf of the first harvest
Answer: D. A sheaf of the first harvest. The first harvest was given back to God.


Q6. True or false: God said the feasts were His appointed feasts, to be proclaimed as holy convocations. (23:2) Hard
Answer: True. The feasts belonged to the LORD, not to human calendars.


Q7. True or false: The Feast of Unleavened Bread was to last seven days. (23:6) Easy
Answer: True. God's people remembered their hasty departure from Egypt.


Q8. Fill in the blank: The Feast of Trumpets was a day of solemn ______ and a memorial blowing of trumpets. (23:24) Medium
Answer: rest. Trumpets called Israel to remember and rest before God.


Leviticus 24
Q1. Fill in the blank: Take fine flour and bake twelve cakes, and set them in two rows on the ______ table before the LORD. (24:6) Easy
Answer: pure. The bread was a standing sign of God's covenant with His people.


Q2. True or false: Moses put the blasphemer under guard until the LORD's decision was made known. (24:12) Hard
Answer: True. God's people sought God's word before judgment.


Q3. Multiple choice: What measure of justice did the law give for injury? (24:19-20) Medium
A) Exile   B) Like for like   C) Double payment   D) Forgiveness only
Answer: B. Like for like. Justice is measured and proportionate.


Q4. True or false: The bread of the Presence was to be replaced every Sabbath. (24:8) Medium
Answer: True. God's table was served with regular faithfulness.


Q5. Multiple choice: What happened to the son of the Israelite woman who blasphemed the Name? (24:10-14) Hard
A) He was made a priest   B) He was forgiven at once   C) He was brought out and stoned by the congregation   D) He was sent to Egypt
Answer: C. He was brought out and stoned by the congregation. The holiness of God's name is protected in the community.


Q6. Multiple choice: What was to be kept burning continually outside the veil? (24:2-3) Easy
A) Incense   B) A candle at the king's house   C) The lamp   D) The altar fire on the mountain
Answer: C. The lamp. Light was kept before the LORD continually.


Q7. Fill in the blank: Under this law, someone who intentionally kills a human being is to be put to ______. (24:17, 21) Medium
Answer: death. The rule concerns taking human life, not every act of striking someone. The separate laws about bodily injury appear in verses 19-20.


Q8. True or false: A man who blasphemed the name of the LORD was to be brought out of the camp and stoned. (24:14) Easy
Answer: True. God's name is holy, and blasphemy is taken seriously.


Leviticus 25
Q1. True or false: The Israelites were not to take interest from a poor brother. (25:36-37) Medium
Answer: True. Generosity to the poor reflects God's mercy.


Q2. True or false: The land was not to be sold permanently, for the land belongs to God. (25:23) Easy
Answer: True. Ownership belonged to God, and families were stewards.


Q3. Multiple choice: In the fiftieth year, what was the trumpet to proclaim? (25:9-10) Easy
A) A tax   B) A new king   C) Liberty throughout the land   D) War
Answer: C. Liberty throughout the land. The jubilee restored families and land to their place.


Q4. Fill in the blank: If your brother becomes poor and cannot support himself, you shall help him, as you would a stranger or ______. (25:35) Medium
Answer: sojourner. God's people care for the poor among them.


Q5. True or false: The jubilee year was to be proclaimed on the Day of Atonement. (25:9) Hard
Answer: True. Atonement and liberty were proclaimed together.


Q6. Multiple choice: What reason does God give for the rules in Leviticus 25? (25:38, 25:55) Hard
A) Kings demand it   B) Land is cheap   C) Priests decide it   D) I brought you out of Egypt and you are My servants
Answer: D. I brought you out of Egypt and you are My servants. Israel's freedom reflects God's deliverance.


Q7. Fill in the blank: In the sabbath year the land was to have a sabbath of ______ to the LORD. (25:4) Easy
Answer: rest. God gave the land rest as part of His care.


Q8. Multiple choice: What was the purpose of the right of redemption when a family member became poor? (25:25) Medium
A) A relative could buy back the property   B) It was given to the priests   C) It was burned   D) The king took it
Answer: A. A relative could buy back the property. God protects families from permanent loss.


Leviticus 26
Q1. True or false: God said He would not abhor His people so as to destroy them utterly or break His covenant. (26:44) Hard
Answer: True. God's covenant faithfulness holds even in judgment.


Q2. Multiple choice: What did God say about the land while His people were exiled? (26:34-35) Hard
A) The land would become a city   B) The land would enjoy its sabbaths   C) The land would be sold   D) The land would be destroyed forever
Answer: B. The land would enjoy its sabbaths. God's sabbath rest for the land is honored even in exile.


Q3. True or false: God promised, "I will be your God, and you shall be My people." (26:12) Easy
Answer: True. The covenant relationship is God's promise.


Q4. Fill in the blank: If you despise My statutes and abhor My rules, I will bring a sword upon you to avenge the ______ of My covenant. (26:25) Medium
Answer: breaking. God takes His covenant seriously.


Q5. Multiple choice: What did God promise to do if His people walked in His statutes? (26:11-12) Easy
A) Give them gold   B) Set His dwelling among them and walk among them   C) Make them kings of the world   D) Send them back to Egypt
Answer: B. Set His dwelling among them and walk among them. God's presence is the greatest blessing.


Q6. True or false: God promised to remember His covenant with Jacob, with Isaac and with Abraham, even in exile. (26:42) Medium
Answer: True. God's faithfulness remains even in judgment.


Q7. Multiple choice: What did God say He would do if His people refused to obey? (26:14-17) Medium
A) Make them wealthy   B) Set His face against them so they were defeated   C) Forgive them at once   D) Ignore them
Answer: B. Set His face against them so they were defeated. Disobedience brings the consequences of the covenant.


Q8. Fill in the blank: If you walk in My statutes and keep My commandments, I will give you rain in its season, and the land shall yield its ______. (26:4) Easy
Answer: produce. Obedience brings blessing to the land.


Leviticus 27
Q1. Fill in the blank: If a man vows the value of a male aged twenty to sixty, the value is fifty ______ of silver. (27:3) Easy
Answer: shekels. Vows were valued with fairness and care.


Q2. Fill in the blank: If a man consecrates his house as holy to the LORD, the owner may redeem it by adding a ______ to its value. (27:15) Medium
Answer: fifth. Redemption required paying the value plus an added fifth.


Q3. True or false: Every tenth animal that passes under the shepherd's rod is holy to the LORD. (27:32) Easy
Answer: True. Even the flock was counted holy to God.


Q4. True or false: A firstborn animal could be consecrated again as a vow, since it was already the LORD's. (27:26) Medium
Answer: False. The firstborn already belonged to the LORD and could not be vowed as a gift.


Q5. Multiple choice: What closing reason does Leviticus 27 give for these holy things? (27:34) Hard
A) They are the priests' customs   B) They come from Egypt   C) They are the king's laws   D) These are the commandments the LORD gave to Moses on Mount Sinai
Answer: D. These are the commandments the LORD gave to Moses on Mount Sinai. The LORD gave these rules to Moses at Mount Sinai.


Q6. Multiple choice: What could not be sold or redeemed once it was devoted to the LORD? (27:28) Medium
A) A devoted thing, which is most holy to the LORD   B) A field   C) A cow   D) A house
Answer: A. A devoted thing, which is most holy to the LORD. Devoted gifts were set apart completely.


Q7. True or false: A firstborn ox or sheep could be presented as a new voluntary dedication in the same way as a house. (27:14-15, 26) Hard
Answer: False. The firstborn already belonged to the LORD, whereas a house could be dedicated and redeemed under the stated terms.


Q8. Multiple choice: What was to be done with a tithe of the land and of the trees? (27:30) Easy
A) It is burned   B) It is sold to the king   C) It is kept by the poor   D) It belongs to the LORD and is holy
Answer: D. It belongs to the LORD and is holy. Tithes acknowledge that everything belongs to God.


Numbers
Numbers 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… to the number of the names, one by one, every male from ______ years old and upward, all who were able to go out to war;”? (1:20, WEB) Medium
A) ninety   B) nineteen   C) sixty   D) twenty
Answer: D. twenty. The passage specifies twenty: “… to the number of the names, one by one, every male from twenty years old and upward, all who were able to go out to war;”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Of the children of ______: Of Ephraim: Elishama the son of Ammihud. Of Manasseh: Gamaliel the son of Pedahzur.”? (1:10, WEB) Easy
A) Zophar   B) Aaron   C) Joseph   D) Cain
Answer: C. Joseph. Numbers 1:10 uses Joseph: “Of the children of Joseph: Of Ephraim: Elishama the son of Ammihud. Of Manasseh: Gamaliel the son of Pedahzur.”


Q3. Multiple choice: Why are the Levites excluded from the military census and assigned to the tabernacle instead? (1:47-53) Hard
A) Their distinct duty is to care for the tabernacle and camp around it.   B) They are not part of Israel.   C) They have been forbidden to worship God.   D) They are too wealthy for military service.
Answer: A. Their distinct duty is to care for the tabernacle and camp around it.. The passage assigns the Levites responsibility for the tabernacle and a protective boundary around it. Their exclusion is a different calling, not a declaration that they are unimportant.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “those who were numbered of them, of the tribe of ______, were fifty-seven thousand four hundred.” (1:31); “Of the children of ______, their generations, by their families, by their fathers' houses, according to the number of the names, from twenty years old and …” (1:36, WEB) Hard
A) Benjamin; Zebulun   B) Zebulun; Benjamin   C) Zebulun; Eleazar   D) Eleazar; Benjamin
Answer: B. Zebulun; Benjamin. Verse 31 reads “those who were numbered of them, of the tribe of Zebulun, were fifty-seven thousand four hundred.”; verse 36 reads “Of the children of Benjamin, their generations, by their families, by their fathers' houses, according to the number of the names, from twenty years old and …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… were called of the congregation, the princes of the ______ of their fathers; they were the ______ of the thousands of Israel.” (1:16, WEB) Medium
Answer: tribes; heads. The wording is “… were called of the congregation, the princes of the tribes of their fathers; they were the heads of the thousands of Israel.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “those who were numbered of them, of the tribe of Gad, were forty-five thousand six hundred fifty.” (1:25, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “those who were numbered of them, of the tribe of Gad, were forty-five thousand six hundred fifty.”


Q7. True or false: The census counted men twenty years old and above who could serve in war. (1:3) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 1:3 records: “from twenty years old and upward, all who are able to go out to war in Israel. You and Aaron shall number them by their divisions.”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “"Take a census of all the congregation of the children of ______, by their families, by their fathers' houses, according to the number of the names, …” (1:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Israel. The cited words are “"Take a census of all the congregation of the children of Israel, by their families, by their fathers' houses, according to the number of the names, …”


Numbers 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “"Those who encamp next to him shall be the tribe of ______: and the prince of the children of Asher shall be Pagiel the son of …” (2:27); “"All who were numbered of the camp of ______ were one hundred fifty-seven thousand six hundred. They shall set out last by their standards."” (2:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: Asher; Dan. The first passage reads “"Those who encamp next to him shall be the tribe of Asher: and the prince of the children of Asher shall be Pagiel the son of …”; the second reads “"All who were numbered of the camp of Dan were one hundred fifty-seven thousand six hundred. They shall set out last by their standards."”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… toward the sunrise shall be of the standard of the camp of ______, according to their divisions: and the prince of the children of Judah shall …” (2:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Judah. The cited words are “… toward the sunrise shall be of the standard of the camp of Judah, according to their divisions: and the prince of the children of Judah shall …”


Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, israel camped around the tent of meeting, arranged by their ______. (2:2) Easy
Answer: standards. Numbers 2:2 records: “"The children of Israel shall encamp every man by his own standard, with the ensigns of their fathers' houses: at a distance from the tent of meeting shall they encamp around it."”


Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… standard of the camp of ______ according to their divisions. The prince of the children of Reuben shall be ______ the son of Shedeur.” (2:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: Reuben; Elizur. The wording is “… standard of the camp of Reuben according to their divisions. The prince of the children of Reuben shall be Elizur the son of Shedeur.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… be the tribe of Manasseh: and the prince of the children of ______ shall be Gamaliel the son of Pedahzur.” (2:20); “His host, and those who were numbered of them, were thirty-five thousand ______ hundred.” (2:23, WEB) Hard
A) Manasseh; four   B) four; Manasseh   C) Manasseh; eighth   D) Moses; four
Answer: A. Manasseh; four. Verse 20 reads “… be the tribe of Manasseh: and the prince of the children of Manasseh shall be Gamaliel the son of Pedahzur.”; verse 23 reads “His host, and those who were numbered of them, were thirty-five thousand four hundred.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… thousand four hundred fifty, according to their hosts. They shall set out eighty.” (2:16, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… thousand four hundred fifty, according to their hosts. They shall set out second.” It says second, not eighty.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “His division, and those who were numbered of it, were ______-four thousand four hundred.”? (2:6, WEB) Easy
A) ninth   B) thousand   C) fifty   D) ninety
Answer: C. fifty. Numbers 2:6 uses fifty: “His division, and those who were numbered of it, were fifty-four thousand four hundred.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “His division, and those who were numbered of them, were fifty-______ thousand three hundred.”? (2:13, WEB) Medium
A) three   B) nine   C) sixty   D) seventh
Answer: B. nine. The passage specifies nine: “His division, and those who were numbered of them, were fifty-nine thousand three hundred.”


Numbers 3
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “These are the names of the sons of ______, the priests who were anointed, whom he consecrated to minister in the priest's office.” (3:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Aaron. The cited words are “These are the names of the sons of Aaron, the priests who were anointed, whom he consecrated to minister in the priest's office.”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the sons of Kohath shall encamp on the south side of the ______.” (3:29); “… number of all the males, from a month old and upward, were ______ thousand two hundred.” (3:34, WEB) Hard
A) six; tabernacle   B) tabernacle; six   C) lampstand; six   D) tabernacle; thousand
Answer: B. tabernacle; six. Verse 29 reads “… the sons of Kohath shall encamp on the south side of the tabernacle.”; verse 34 reads “… number of all the males, from a month old and upward, were six thousand two hundred.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… are wholly given to him on the behalf of the children of ______.”? (3:9, WEB) Easy
A) Jesse   B) Josiah   C) Israel   D) Naomi
Answer: C. Israel. Numbers 3:9 uses Israel: “… are wholly given to him on the behalf of the children of Israel.”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The sons of Kohath by their families: Amram, and Izhar, ______, and Uzziel.”? (3:19, WEB) Medium
A) Gilgal   B) Israel   C) Persia   D) Hebron
Answer: D. Hebron. The passage specifies Hebron: “The sons of Kohath by their families: Amram, and Izhar, Hebron, and Uzziel.”


Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The daughters of the fathers' house of the Gershonites shall be Eliasaph the son of Lael.” (3:24, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The prince of the fathers' house of the Gershonites shall be Eliasaph the son of Lael.” It says prince, not daughters.


Q6. True or false: The Levites were taken in place of the firstborn of Israel. (3:12) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 3:12 records: “"Behold, I have taken the Levites from among the children of Israel instead of all the firstborn who open the womb among the children of Israel; and the Levites shall be mine:”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “"______ the children of ______ by their fathers' houses, by their families: every male from a month old and upward shall you number."” (3:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: Number; Levi. The wording is “"Number the children of Levi by their fathers' houses, by their families: every male from a month old and upward shall you number."” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “All who were numbered of the Levites, whom ______ and Aaron numbered at the commandment of Yahweh, by their families, all the males from a month …” (3:39); “… instead of all the firstborn among the children of Israel, and the ______ of the Levites instead of their cattle; and the Levites shall be mine. …” (3:45, WEB) Hard
Answer: Moses; cattle. The first passage reads “All who were numbered of the Levites, whom Moses and Aaron numbered at the commandment of Yahweh, by their families, all the males from a month …”; the second reads “… instead of all the firstborn among the children of Israel, and the cattle of the Levites instead of their cattle; and the Levites shall be mine. …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Numbers 4
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… live, and not die, when they approach to the most holy things: ______ and his sons shall go in, and appoint them everyone to his service …”? (4:19, WEB) Medium
A) Amos   B) Mordecai   C) Jacob   D) Aaron
Answer: D. Aaron. The passage specifies Aaron: “… live, and not die, when they approach to the most holy things: Aaron and his sons shall go in, and appoint them everyone to his service …”


Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “from thirty years old and upward until fifty years old shall you number them; all who enter in to wait on the service, to do the …” (4:23, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “from thirty years old and upward until fifty years old shall you number them; all who enter in to wait on the service, to do the …”


Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… Tent of Meeting: and their charge shall be under the hand of ______ the son of Aaron the priest.” (4:28); “… of Meeting, under the hand of Ithamar the son of Aaron the ______."” (4:33, WEB) Hard
A) Ithamar; priest   B) Nehemiah; priest   C) priest; Ithamar   D) Ithamar; prince
Answer: A. Ithamar; priest. Verse 28 reads “… Tent of Meeting: and their charge shall be under the hand of Ithamar the son of Aaron the priest.”; verse 33 reads “… of Meeting, under the hand of Ithamar the son of Aaron the priest."”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… whom Moses and Aaron numbered according to the commandment of Yahweh by ______.” (4:37); “… everyone who entered into the service, for work in the Tent of ______,” (4:43, WEB) Hard
Answer: Moses; Meeting. The first passage reads “… whom Moses and Aaron numbered according to the commandment of Yahweh by Moses.”; the second reads “… everyone who entered into the service, for work in the Tent of Meeting,”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Answer: fifty. The cited words are “from thirty years old and upward even until fifty years old, all who enter into the service, to do the work in the Tent of Meeting.”

Answer: fire; altar. The wording is “… they minister about it, the fire pans, the flesh hooks, the shovels, and the basins; all the vessels of the altar; and they shall spread on …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “They shall take a blue cloth, and cover the lampstand of the ______, and its lamps, and its snuffers, and its snuff dishes, and all its …”? (4:9, WEB) Easy

Answer: A. light. Numbers 4:9 uses light: “They shall take a blue cloth, and cover the lampstand of the light, and its lamps, and its snuffers, and its snuff dishes, and all its …”

Answer: holy. Numbers 4:4 records: “This is the service of the sons of Kohath in the Tent of Meeting, the most holy things.”


Numbers 5
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “"Speak to the children of ______, and tell them: If any man's wife goes astray, and is unfaithful to him,”? (5:12, WEB) Medium
A) Nebuchadnezzar   B) Micah   C) Israel   D) Abraham
Answer: C. Israel. The passage specifies Israel: “"Speak to the children of Israel, and tell them: If any man's wife goes astray, and is unfaithful to him,”


Q2. True or false: A person who was unclean was sent outside the camp. (5:2) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 5:2 records: “"Command the children of Israel that they put out of the camp every leper, and everyone who has an issue, and whoever is unclean by the dead.”


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… is a meal offering of jealousy, a meal offering of memorial, bringing transgressions to memory.” (5:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… is a meal offering of jealousy, a meal offering of memorial, bringing iniquity to memory.” It says iniquity, not transgressions.


Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Both male and female shall you put out, ______ of the camp shall you put them; that they not defile their camp, in the midst of …” (5:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: outside. The cited words are “Both male and female shall you put out, outside of the camp shall you put them; that they not defile their camp, in the midst of …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Every heave ______ of all the ______ things of the children of Israel, which they present to the priest, shall be his.” (5:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: offering; holy. The wording is “Every heave offering of all the holy things of the children of Israel, which they present to the priest, shall be his.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. Multiple choice: Who administers the ritual when a husband suspects adultery without sufficient evidence? (5:12-31) Hard
A) The king automatically declares the wife guilty.   B) The husband may impose any punishment he chooses.   C) The husband is told to ignore every accusation.   D) The priest brings the matter before the LORD under the prescribed procedure.
Answer: D. The priest brings the matter before the LORD under the prescribed procedure.. The text regulates an ancient ordeal; it does not authorize a suspicious husband to punish his wife privately. Its details are debated and must be read in their ancient covenant setting.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… to the children of Israel: When a man or woman commits any ______ that men commit, so as to trespass against Yahweh, and that soul is …”? (5:6, WEB) Easy
A) idolatry   B) wickedness   C) sin   D) transgressions
Answer: C. sin. Numbers 5:6 uses sin: “… to the children of Israel: When a man or woman commits any sin that men commit, so as to trespass against Yahweh, and that soul is …”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He shall make the woman drink the water of bitterness that ______ the curse; and the water that causes the curse shall enter into her and …” (5:24); “… committed a trespass against her husband, that the water that causes the ______ will enter into her and become bitter, and her body will swell, and …” (5:27, WEB) Hard
Answer: causes; curse. The first passage reads “He shall make the woman drink the water of bitterness that causes the curse; and the water that causes the curse shall enter into her and …”; the second reads “… committed a trespass against her husband, that the water that causes the curse will enter into her and become bitter, and her body will swell, and …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Numbers 6
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… for his father, or for his ______, for his brother, or for his sister, when they die; because his ______ to God is on his head.” (6:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: mother; separation. The wording is “… for his father, or for his mother, for his brother, or for his sister, when they die; because his separation to God is on his head.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q2. Multiple choice: How do the words after the priestly blessing explain its purpose? (6:22-27) Hard
A) The priests place God's name on Israel, and God blesses them.   B) The words guarantee wealth to every Israelite.   C) The priests replace God as the source of blessing.   D) Only Aaron may receive God's name.
Answer: A. The priests place God's name on Israel, and God blesses them.. Verse 27 distinguishes the priests' spoken blessing from God's action. The blessing invokes God's care rather than treating a formula as independent magical power.


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “"This is the law of the Nazirite: when the days of his separation are fulfilled, he shall be brought to the door of the Tent of …” (6:13, WEB) Medium

You shall tell them,” (6:23, WEB) Hard

You shall tell them,”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… down and talk with you there: and I will take of the manna which is on you, and will put it on them; and they shall …” (11:17, WEB) Medium

Q4. Multiple choice: How does Moses respond when Joshua wants Eldad and Medad stopped from prophesying? (11:26-30) Hard
A) He wishes that all the LORD's people were prophets and received His Spirit.   B) He orders their execution.   C) He denies that God can work outside his tent.   D) He makes Joshua the source of their gift.
Answer: A. He wishes that all the LORD's people were prophets and received His Spirit.. Moses refuses to defend his status through jealousy. His response values God's work among the people more than control of the prophetic gift.

Q6. True or false: Moses complained that the burden of the people was too heavy for him. (11:14) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 11:14 records: “I am not able to bear all this people alone, because it is too heavy for me.”


Q7. Answer: fire. The cited words are “The people cried to Moses; and Moses prayed to Yahweh, and the fire abated.”


Numbers 12
Q1. True or false: Moses refuses to pray for Miriam after she and Aaron criticize him. (12:1-13) Hard
Answer: False. Moses cries out for her healing in verse 13. God's response to the dispute concerns Moses' distinctive role; the story does not justify prejudice against a people group.


Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, miriam and Aaron spoke against Moses because of his Cushite ______. (12:1) Easy
Answer: wife. Numbers 12:1 records: “Miriam and Aaron spoke against Moses because of the Cushite woman whom he had married; for he had married a Cushite woman.”


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The cloud removed from over the Tent; and, behold, Miriam was mother's, as white as snow: and Aaron looked at Miriam, and, behold, she was leprous.” (12:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The cloud removed from over the Tent; and, behold, Miriam was leprous, as white as snow: and Aaron looked at Miriam, and, behold, she was leprous.” It says leprous, not mother's.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… he see: why then were you not afraid to speak against my ______, against Moses?”? (12:8, WEB) Medium
A) wives   B) servant   C) daughters   D) father
Answer: B. servant. The passage specifies servant: “… he see: why then were you not afraid to speak against my servant, against Moses?”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Yahweh spoke suddenly to Moses, and to Aaron, and to ______, Come out you three to the tent of meeting. They three came out.” (12:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: Miriam. The cited words are “Yahweh spoke suddenly to Moses, and to Aaron, and to Miriam, Come out you three to the tent of meeting. They three came out.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Yahweh came down in a pillar of ______, and stood at the door of the Tent, and called ______ and Miriam; and they both came forth.” (12:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: cloud; Aaron. The wording is “Yahweh came down in a pillar of cloud, and stood at the door of the Tent, and called Aaron and Miriam; and they both came forth.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Now the man ______ was very humble, above all the men who were on the surface of the earth.”? (12:3, WEB) Easy
A) Nehemiah   B) Zebulun   C) Issachar   D) Moses
Answer: D. Moses. Numbers 12:3 uses Moses: “Now the man Moses was very humble, above all the men who were on the surface of the earth.”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… her father had but spit in her face, shouldn't she be ashamed ______ days? let her be shut up outside of the camp seven days, and …” (12:14); “Miriam was shut up ______ of the camp seven days: and the people didn't travel until Miriam was brought in again.” (12:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: seven; outside. The first passage reads “… her father had but spit in her face, shouldn't she be ashamed seven days? let her be shut up outside of the camp seven days, and …”; the second reads “Miriam was shut up outside of the camp seven days: and the people didn't travel until Miriam was brought in again.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Numbers 14
Q1. Multiple choice: How do God's forgiveness and the wilderness judgment appear together? (14:19-24) Hard
A) Moses' prayer is never answered.   B) Forgiveness means no one faces any consequence.   C) God forgives, yet the unbelieving generation still faces the stated consequence.   D) Caleb is praised for agreeing with the fearful majority.
Answer: C. God forgives, yet the unbelieving generation still faces the stated consequence.. Verses 20-23 place pardon beside the loss of entry into the land. The course similarly distinguishes forgiveness from the removal of every consequence.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “But all the congregation bade stone them with stones. The ______ of Yahweh ______ in the tent of meeting to all the children of Israel.” (14:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: glory; appeared. The wording is “But all the congregation bade stone them with stones. The glory of Yahweh appeared in the tent of meeting to all the children of Israel.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… dwell in the valley: tomorrow turn you, and get you into the ______ by the way to the Red Sea.” (14:25); “… concerning which I swore that I would make you dwell therein, save ______ the son of Jephunneh, and Joshua the son of Nun.” (14:30, WEB) Hard
A) Caleb; wilderness   B) wilderness; Caleb   C) valley; Caleb   D) wilderness; Judah
Answer: B. wilderness; Caleb. Verse 25 reads “… dwell in the valley: tomorrow turn you, and get you into the wilderness by the way to the Red Sea.”; verse 30 reads “… concerning which I swore that I would make you dwell therein, save Caleb the son of Jephunneh, and Joshua the son of Nun.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “If Yahweh ______ in us, then he will bring us into this land, and give it to us; a land which flows with milk and honey.”? (14:8, WEB) Easy
A) delight   B) know   C) covenant   D) nation
Answer: A. delight. Numbers 14:8 uses delight: “If Yahweh delight in us, then he will bring us into this land, and give it to us; a land which flows with milk and honey.”


Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… worked in Egypt and in the wilderness, yet have tempted me these four times, and have not listened to my voice;” (14:22, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… worked in Egypt and in the wilderness, yet have tempted me these ten times, and have not listened to my voice;” It says ten, not four.


Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… be a prey: wouldn't it be better for us to return into ______?” (14:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Egypt. The cited words are “… be a prey: wouldn't it be better for us to return into Egypt?”


Q7. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the LORD said Caleb had a different spirit and had ______ followed Him. (14:24) Easy
Answer: wholly. Numbers 14:24 records: “but my servant Caleb, because he had another spirit with him, and has followed me fully, him will I bring into the land into which he went; and his seed shall possess it.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Yahweh is slow to anger, and abundant in loving kindness, forgiving ______ and disobedience; and that will by no means clear the guilty, visiting the iniquity …”? (14:18, WEB) Medium
A) iniquity   B) transgressions   C) disobedience   D) deceit
Answer: A. iniquity. The passage specifies iniquity: “Yahweh is slow to anger, and abundant in loving kindness, forgiving iniquity and disobedience; and that will by no means clear the guilty, visiting the iniquity …”


Numbers 15
Q1. True or false: A man gathering wood on the Sabbath was put to death. (15:32-36) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 15:32 records: “While the children of Israel were in the wilderness, they found a man gathering sticks on the Sabbath day.”


Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “and will make an offering by ______ to Yahweh, a burnt offering, or a sacrifice, to accomplish a vow, or as a freewill-offering, or in your …” (15:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: fire. The cited words are “and will make an offering by fire to Yahweh, a burnt offering, or a sacrifice, to accomplish a vow, or as a freewill-offering, or in your …”


Q3. Multiple choice: What distinction does the chapter draw between unintentional wrongdoing and defiant wrongdoing? (15:22-31) Hard
A) It provides offerings for unintentional sin and separately condemns deliberate, high-handed defiance.   B) It says all wrongdoing is accidental.   C) It makes wealth the test of guilt.   D) It permits defiance as long as an offering is large.
Answer: A. It provides offerings for unintentional sin and separately condemns deliberate, high-handed defiance.. The distinction concerns the posture and circumstances of the offense in Israel's covenant law. It should not be flattened into a claim that every knowing sin is beyond all possible repentance.


Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “and you shall offer for the drink-offering ______ a hin of wine, for an ______ made by fire, of a sweet savor to Yahweh.” (15:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: half; offering. The wording is “and you shall offer for the drink-offering half a hin of wine, for an offering made by fire, of a sweet savor to Yahweh.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the drink-offering you shall offer the third part of a hin of ______, of a sweet savor to Yahweh.”? (15:7, WEB) Easy
A) grain   B) wine   C) olives   D) salt
Answer: B. wine. Numbers 15:7 uses wine: “… the drink-offering you shall offer the third part of a hin of wine, of a sweet savor to Yahweh.”


Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Of the first of your dough you shall offer up a cake for a heave-offering: as the heave-offering of the threshing floor, so shall you heave …” (15:20, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Of the first of your dough you shall offer up a cake for a heave-offering: as the heave-offering of the threshing floor, so shall you heave …”


Q7. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, moses struck the rock with his ______ to bring water. (20:11) Easy
Answer: staff. Numbers 20:11 records: “Moses lifted up his hand, and struck the rock with his rod twice: and water came forth abundantly, and the congregation drank, and their cattle.”


Q3. Multiple choice: Why does God say Moses and Aaron will not bring the assembly into the land? (20:7-13) Hard
A) The rock was outside God's authority.   B) They did not trust God so as to uphold His holiness before Israel.   C) Aaron refused to leave Egypt.   D) The people never received any water.
Answer: B. They did not trust God so as to uphold His holiness before Israel.. Verse 12 supplies God's stated reason. The narrative mentions the command to speak and Moses' striking, but the verdict should be explained using the reason the text actually gives.


Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Moses sent messengers from Kadesh to the king of Edom, Thus says your brother Israel, You know all the travail that has happened to us:” (20:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Moses sent messengers from Kadesh to the king of Edom, Thus says your brother Israel, You know all the travail that has happened to us:”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… for the congregation: and they assembled themselves together against Moses and against ______.” (20:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Aaron. The cited words are “… for the congregation: and they assembled themselves together against Moses and against Aaron.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Take the ______, and assemble the congregation, you, and ______ your brother, and speak you to the rock before their eyes, that it give forth its …” (20:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: rod; Aaron. The wording is “Take the rod, and assemble the congregation, you, and Aaron your brother, and speak you to the rock before their eyes, that it give forth its …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… pass through field or through vineyard, neither will we drink of the ______ of the wells: we will go along the king's highway; we will not …” (20:17); “… the highway; and if we drink of your water, I and my ______, then will I give the price of it: let me only, without doing …” (20:19, WEB) Hard
A) water; rebelled   B) water; cattle   C) cattle; water   D) barley; cattle
Answer: B. water; cattle. Verse 17 reads “… pass through field or through vineyard, neither will we drink of the water of the wells: we will go along the king's highway; we will not …”; verse 19 reads “… the highway; and if we drink of your water, I and my cattle, then will I give the price of it: let me only, without doing …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… of the tent of meeting, and fell on their faces: and the ______ of Yahweh appeared to them.”? (20:6, WEB) Easy
A) shame   B) glory   C) grief   D) wisdom
Answer: B. glory. Numbers 20:6 uses glory: “… of the tent of meeting, and fell on their faces: and the glory of Yahweh appeared to them.”


Numbers 21
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… bread, and there is no water; and our soul loathes this light ______.”? (21:5, WEB) Easy
A) bread   B) salt   C) flour   D) figs
Answer: A. bread. Numbers 21:5 uses bread: “… bread, and there is no water; and our soul loathes this light bread.”


Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Israel vowed a vow to Yahweh, and said, If you will indeed ______ this people into my hand, then I will utterly destroy their cities.” (21:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: deliver. The cited words are “Israel vowed a vow to Yahweh, and said, If you will indeed deliver this people into my hand, then I will utterly destroy their cities.”


Q3. Multiple choice: Why must the bronze serpent be distinguished from an object with power of its own? (21:4-9; 2 Kings 18:4) Hard
A) Israel is commanded to worship every serpent.   B) Bronze always heals illness.   C) God appointed it in this episode, and it was later destroyed when people burned incense to it.   D) The object replaces God permanently.
Answer: C. God appointed it in this episode, and it was later destroyed when people burned incense to it.. Numbers locates rescue in God's provision. Second Kings shows that a God-given object could later become an idol; John 3:14-15 draws a Christian connection to Jesus being lifted up.


Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The children of ______ traveled, and encamped in ______.” (21:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: Israel; Oboth. The wording is “The children of Israel traveled, and encamped in Oboth.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… the border of the Amorites: for the Arnon is the border of ______, between Moab and the Amorites.”? (21:13, WEB) Medium
A) Tarshish   B) Moab   C) Assyria   D) Sinai
Answer: B. Moab. The passage specifies Moab: “… the border of the Amorites: for the Arnon is the border of Moab, between Moab and the Amorites.”


Q6. True or false: Moses made a bronze serpent so that bitten people could look and live. (21:6-9) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 21:6 records: “Yahweh sent fiery serpents among the people, and they bit the people; and much people of Israel died.”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “For a ______ is gone out of Heshbon, A flame from the city of Sihon: It has devoured Ar of Moab, The lords of the high …” (21:28); “We have shot at them; Heshbon is ______ even to Dibon, We have laid waste even to Nophah, Which reaches to Medeba.” (21:30, WEB) Hard
Answer: fire; perished. The first passage reads “For a fire is gone out of Heshbon, A flame from the city of Sihon: It has devoured Ar of Moab, The lords of the high …”; the second reads “We have shot at them; Heshbon is perished even to Dibon, We have laid waste even to Nophah, Which reaches to Medeba.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Then sang Israel this song: Spring up, well; sing you to it:” (21:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Then sang Israel this song: Spring up, well; sing you to it:”


Numbers 22
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… and they came to Balaam, and spoke to him the words of ______.”? (22:7, WEB) Easy
A) Joseph   B) Balak   C) Mordecai   D) Benjamin
Answer: B. Balak. Numbers 22:7 uses Balak: “… and they came to Balaam, and spoke to him the words of Balak.”


Q2. Multiple choice: What irony appears in Balaam's encounter on the road? (22:22-35) Hard
A) Balaam sees everything while the donkey sees nothing.   B) The donkey hires Balaam.   C) Balak appears as the angel.   D) The donkey sees the angel before the seer does.
Answer: D. The donkey sees the angel before the seer does.. The animal perceives the danger while Balaam repeatedly strikes it. God must open Balaam's eyes, showing the limits of the seer's perception.


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “God came to Balaam at night, and said to him, If the men are come to call you, rise up, go with them; but only the …” (22:20, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “God came to Balaam at night, and said to him, If the men are come to call you, rise up, go with them; but only the …”


Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, balaam's donkey saw the angel of the LORD standing in the ______. (22:23) Easy
Answer: road. Numbers 22:23 records: “… the angel of Yahweh standing in the way, with his sword drawn in his hand; and the donkey turned aside out of the way, and went into the field: and Balaam struck the donkey, to turn …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the people that is come out of ______, it covers the surface of the earth: now, come ______ me them; peradventure I shall be able to …” (22:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: Egypt; curse. The wording is “… the people that is come out of Egypt, it covers the surface of the earth: now, come curse me them; peradventure I shall be able to …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… servants of Balak, If Balak would give me his house full of ______ and gold, I can't go beyond the word of Yahweh my God, to …”? (22:18, WEB) Medium
A) clay   B) silver   C) bronze   D) iron
Answer: B. silver. The passage specifies silver: “… servants of Balak, If Balak would give me his house full of silver and gold, I can't go beyond the word of Yahweh my God, to …”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… of Yahweh said to him, Why have you struck your donkey these ______ times? behold, I am come forth for an adversary, because your way is …” (22:32); “… to meet him to the City of Moab, which is on the ______ of the Arnon, which is in the utmost part of the border.” (22:36, WEB) Hard
Answer: three; border. The first passage reads “… of Yahweh said to him, Why have you struck your donkey these three times? behold, I am come forth for an adversary, because your way is …”; the second reads “… to meet him to the City of Moab, which is on the border of the Arnon, which is in the utmost part of the border.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Moab was sore afraid of the people, because they were many: and ______ was distressed because of the children of Israel.” (22:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Moab. The cited words are “Moab was sore afraid of the people, because they were many: and Moab was distressed because of the children of Israel.”


Numbers 23
Q1. Multiple choice: What is the point of saying that God is not a man who lies in Balaam's oracle? (23:19-20) Hard
A) God has promised to bless every action Israel takes.   B) God's declared blessing cannot be overturned by Balak's attempt to obtain a curse.   C) Balak can buy any prophecy he wants.   D) God is admitting His promise was false.
Answer: B. God's declared blessing cannot be overturned by Balak's attempt to obtain a curse.. The statement concerns God's truthfulness and commitment in this oracle. It should be read alongside passages about God relenting, rather than used to erase those passages.


Q2. True or false: Balaam blessed Israel instead of cursing them. (23:11) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 23:11 records: “Balak said to Balaam, What have you done to me? I took you to curse my enemies, and, behold, you have blessed them altogether.”


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “He said to Balak, Stand here by your burnt praise, while I meet Yahweh yonder.” (23:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “He said to Balak, Stand here by your burnt offering, while I meet Yahweh yonder.” It says offering, not praise.


Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Who can count the dust of ______, Or number the ______ part of Israel? Let me die the death of the righteous, Let my last end …” (23:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: Jacob; fourth. The wording is “Who can count the dust of Jacob, Or number the fourth part of Israel? Let me die the death of the righteous, Let my last end …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… and I have offered up a bull and a ram on every ______.”? (23:4, WEB) Easy
A) houses   B) altar   C) tent   D) ark
Answer: B. altar. Numbers 23:4 uses altar: “… and I have offered up a bull and a ram on every altar.”


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… into the field of Zophim, to the top of Pisgah, and built ______ altars, and offered up a bull and a ram on every altar.”? (23:14, WEB) Medium
A) hundred   B) five   C) twenty   D) seven
Answer: D. seven. The passage specifies seven: “… into the field of Zophim, to the top of Pisgah, and built seven altars, and offered up a bull and a ram on every altar.”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… shall not lie down until he eat of the prey, Drink the ______ of the slain.” (23:24); “Balak said to ______, Come now, I will take you to another place; peradventure it will please God that you may curse me them from there.” (23:27, WEB) Hard
Answer: blood; Balaam. The first passage reads “… shall not lie down until he eat of the prey, Drink the blood of the slain.”; the second reads “Balak said to Balaam, Come now, I will take you to another place; peradventure it will please God that you may curse me them from there.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Balak did as Balaam had spoken; and ______ and Balaam offered on every altar a bull and a ram.” (23:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Balak. The cited words are “Balak did as Balaam had spoken; and Balak and Balaam offered on every altar a bull and a ram.”


Numbers 24
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “If Balak would give me his house full of bronze and gold, I can't go beyond the word of Yahweh, to do either good or bad …” (24:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “If Balak would give me his house full of silver and gold, I can't go beyond the word of Yahweh, to do either good or bad …” It says silver, not bronze.


Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He says, who hears the words of God, Who ______ the vision of the Almighty, Falling down, and having his eyes open:”? (24:4, WEB) Easy
A) wilderness   B) sees   C) blesses   D) Knows
Answer: B. sees. Numbers 24:4 uses sees: “He says, who hears the words of God, Who sees the vision of the Almighty, Falling down, and having his eyes open:”


Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ lifted up his eyes, and he saw Israel dwelling according to their tribes; and the Spirit of God came on him.” (24:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Balaam. The cited words are “Balaam lifted up his eyes, and he saw Israel dwelling according to their tribes; and the Spirit of God came on him.”


Q4. Multiple choice: Why is the star-and-scepter language more than a description of the campsite? (24:17-19) Hard
A) It predicts that all stars will disappear that night.   B) It portrays a future ruler's authority and victory.   C) It gives instructions for building tents.   D) It names Balaam as Israel's king.
Answer: B. It portrays a future ruler's authority and victory.. The scepter is royal imagery, and the oracle looks beyond Balaam's immediate moment. Christians often read it messianically, while interpreters discuss its nearer royal setting.


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “______ shall flow from his ______, His seed shall be in many waters, His king shall be higher than Agag, His kingdom shall be exalted.” (24:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Water; buckets. The wording is “Water shall flow from his buckets, His seed shall be in many waters, His king shall be higher than Agag, His kingdom shall be exalted.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… anger was kindled against Balaam, and he struck his hands together; and ______ said to Balaam, I called you to curse my enemies, and, behold, you …”? (24:10, WEB) Medium
A) Dan   B) Jeremiah   C) Balak   D) Manasseh
Answer: C. Balak. The passage specifies Balak: “… anger was kindled against Balaam, and he struck his hands together; and Balak said to Balaam, I called you to curse my enemies, and, behold, you …”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… took up his parable, and said, Amalek was the first of the ______; But his latter end shall come to destruction.” (24:20); “Nevertheless Kain shall be wasted, Until ______ shall carry you away captive.” (24:22, WEB) Hard
Answer: nations; Asshur. The first passage reads “… took up his parable, and said, Amalek was the first of the nations; But his latter end shall come to destruction.”; the second reads “Nevertheless Kain shall be wasted, Until Asshur shall carry you away captive.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, balaam said, How lovely are your tents, O ______. (24:5) Easy
Answer: Jacob. Numbers 24:5 records: “How goodly are your tents, Jacob, Your tents, Israel!”


Numbers 25
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Those who died by the plague were twenty-four thousand.” (25:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Those who died by the plague were twenty-four thousand.”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the sun, that the fierce anger of Yahweh may turn away from ______.”? (25:4, WEB) Easy
A) Gomer   B) Israel   C) Esther   D) Noah
Answer: B. Israel. Numbers 25:4 uses Israel: “… the sun, that the fierce anger of Yahweh may turn away from Israel.”


Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “______ said to the judges of Israel, Kill you everyone his men who have ______ themselves to Baal Peor.” (25:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: Moses; joined. The wording is “Moses said to the judges of Israel, Kill you everyone his men who have joined themselves to Baal Peor.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q4. True or false: Phinehas stopped the plague by acting against the sin at Baal of Peor. (25:7-8) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 25:7 records: “When Phinehas, the son of Eleazar, the son of Aaron the priest, saw it, he rose up from the midst of the congregation, and took a spear in his hand;”


Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… of all the congregation of the children of Israel, while they were ______ at the door of the tent of meeting.”? (25:6, WEB) Medium
A) joy   B) priests   C) weeping   D) righteous
Answer: C. weeping. The passage specifies weeping: “… of all the congregation of the children of Israel, while they were weeping at the door of the tent of meeting.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “for they called the people to the ______ of their gods; and the people ate, and bowed down to their gods.” (25:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: sacrifices. The cited words are “for they called the people to the sacrifices of their gods; and the people ate, and bowed down to their gods.”


Q7. True or false: The judgment at Peor gives every later reader permission to carry out private religious violence. (25:1-13) Hard
Answer: False. The chapter narrates a specific judgment and covenant episode in ancient Israel. Description of Phinehas' act is not a general commission for readers to attack people; the course insists on distinguishing description from commands to all people.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… man of Israel that was slain, who was slain with the Midianite ______, was Zimri, the son of Salu, a prince of a fathers' house among …” (25:14); “The name of the Midianite woman who was slain was Cozbi, the ______ of Zur; he was head of the people of a fathers' house in …” (25:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: woman; daughter. The first passage reads “… man of Israel that was slain, who was slain with the Midianite woman, was Zimri, the son of Salu, a prince of a fathers' house among …”; the second reads “The name of the Midianite woman who was slain was Cozbi, the daughter of Zur; he was head of the people of a fathers' house in …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Numbers 26
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The sons of ______ after their families: of Tola, the family of the Tolaites; of Puvah, the family of the Punites;”? (26:23, WEB) Medium

Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… numbered of them, thirty-two thousand five hundred. These are the sons of ______ after their families.” (26:37); “… Shuhamites, according to those who were numbered of them, were sixty-four thousand ______ hundred.” (26:43, WEB) Hard
A) four; Joseph   B) Joseph; ninety   C) Obed; four   D) Joseph; four
Answer: D. Joseph; four. Verse 37 reads “… numbered of them, thirty-two thousand five hundred. These are the sons of Joseph after their families.”; verse 43 reads “… Shuhamites, according to those who were numbered of them, were sixty-four thousand four hundred.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “These are the sons of Moab: of Iezer, the family of the Iezerites; of Helek, the family of the Helekites;” (26:30, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “These are the sons of Gilead: of Iezer, the family of the Iezerites; of Helek, the family of the Helekites;” It says Gilead, not Moab.

Q7. Q8. Fill in the blank: The second census again counted men from twenty years old who were able to go to ______. (26:2) Easy
Answer: war. Verse 2 defines the group counted. The chapter later compares this census with the generation counted at Sinai.


Numbers 27
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… a man die, and have no son, then you shall cause his ______ to pass to his daughter.” (27:8, WEB) Easy
Answer: inheritance. The cited words are “… a man die, and have no son, then you shall cause his inheritance to pass to his daughter.”


Q2. Multiple choice: How does the response to Zelophehad's daughters show the handling of a new inheritance case? (27:1-11) Hard
A) Moses gives their land to Pharaoh.   B) Moses brings the case before God, who affirms their claim and gives a rule.   C) No rule follows the decision.   D) Their claim is dismissed without a hearing.
Answer: B. Moses brings the case before God, who affirms their claim and gives a rule.. The daughters present a reasoned appeal, and the ruling protects their family's inheritance. Their case becomes guidance for similar situations.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… in the wilderness of Zin, in the strife of the congregation, to ______ me at the waters before their eyes. (These are the waters of Meribah …” (27:14); “Let Yahweh, the God of the ______ of all flesh, appoint a man over the congregation,” (27:16, WEB) Hard
A) hand; spirits   B) sanctify; pass   C) spirits; sanctify   D) sanctify; spirits
Answer: D. sanctify; spirits. Verse 14 reads “… in the wilderness of Zin, in the strife of the congregation, to sanctify me at the waters before their eyes. (These are the waters of Meribah …”; verse 16 reads “Let Yahweh, the God of the spirits of all flesh, appoint a man over the congregation,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q4. True or false: Zelophehad's daughters asked for an inheritance because their father had no sons. (27:1-7) Easy
Answer: True. Numbers 27:1 records: “… Zelophehad, the son of Hepher, the son of Gilead, the son of Machir, the son of Manasseh, of the families of Manasseh the son of Joseph; and these are the names of his daughters: Mahlah, Noah, …”


Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… and see the land which I have given to the children of Ham.” (27:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… and see the land which I have given to the children of Israel.” It says Israel, not Ham.


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… he have no brothers, then you shall give his inheritance to his ______ brothers.”? (27:10, WEB) Medium
A) Moses   B) pass   C) families   D) father's
Answer: D. father's. The passage specifies father's: “… he have no brothers, then you shall give his inheritance to his father's brothers.”


Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… to the children of Israel a statute and ordinance, as Yahweh commanded ______.”? (27:11, WEB) Easy
A) Moses   B) Abram   C) Zophar   D) Noah
Answer: A. Moses. Numbers 27:11 uses Moses: “… to the children of Israel a statute and ordinance, as Yahweh commanded Moses.”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “If he have no ______, then you shall give his inheritance to his ______.” (27:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: daughter; brothers. The wording is “If he have no daughter, then you shall give his inheritance to his brothers.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Numbers 28

Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You shall tell them, This is the offering made by ______ which you shall offer to Yahweh: he-lambs a year old without blemish, two day by …” (28:3, WEB) Easy

Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “One male goat for a sin-offering to Yahweh; it shall be offered besides the continual burnt offering, and the drink-offering of it.” (28:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “One male goat for a sin-offering to Yahweh; it shall be offered besides the continual burnt offering, and the drink-offering of it.”


Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “On the ______ day two he-lambs a year old without blemish, and two ______ parts of an ephah of fine flour for a meal-offering, mixed with …” (28:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Sabbath; tenth. The wording is “On the Sabbath day two he-lambs a year old without blemish, and two tenth parts of an ephah of fine flour for a meal-offering, mixed with …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “It is a continual burnt offering, which was ordained in Mount ______ for a sweet savor, an offering made by fire to Yahweh.”? (28:6, WEB) Easy
A) Jericho   B) Gilead   C) Sinai   D) Canaan
Answer: C. Sinai. Numbers 28:6 uses Sinai: “It is a continual burnt offering, which was ordained in Mount Sinai for a sweet savor, an offering made by fire to Yahweh.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Yahweh: it shall be offered besides the continual burnt offering, and the drink-______ of it.” (28:24); “but you shall ______ a burnt offering for a sweet savor to Yahweh: two young bulls, one ram, seven he-lambs a year old;” (28:27, WEB) Hard
Answer: offering; offer. The first passage reads “… Yahweh: it shall be offered besides the continual burnt offering, and the drink-offering of it.”; the second reads “but you shall offer a burnt offering for a sweet savor to Yahweh: two young bulls, one ram, seven he-lambs a year old;”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

A) olives   B) wine   C) oil   D) wheat

Answer: B. seven; lambs. Numbers 31
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… places in which they lived, and all their encampments, they burnt with ______.”? (31:10, WEB) Easy
A) touched   B) princes   C) fire   D) Phinehas
Answer: C. fire. Numbers 31:10 uses fire: “… places in which they lived, and all their encampments, they burnt with fire.”


Q2. True or false: This account of war against Midian functions as a standing command for Christians to attack other religious groups. (31:1-8) Hard
Answer: False. The passage describes a particular ancient campaign connected with the Peor episode. Its difficult commands must be addressed in context, not converted into permission for present-day religious violence.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… war who went to the battle, This is the statute of the ______ which Yahweh has commanded Moses:”? (31:21, WEB) Medium

Answer: D. law. The passage specifies law: “… war who went to the battle, This is the statute of the law which Yahweh has commanded Moses:”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… children of Israel's half, you shall take one drawn out of every ______, of the persons, of the oxen, of the donkeys, and of the flocks, …” (31:30); “… of those who went out to war, was in number three hundred ______-seven thousand five hundred sheep:” (31:36, WEB) Hard
A) fifty; thirty   B) fifty; fourteen   C) fifth; thirty   D) thirty; fifty
Answer: A. fifty; thirty. Verse 30 reads “… children of Israel's half, you shall take one drawn out of every fifty, of the persons, of the oxen, of the donkeys, and of the flocks, …”; verse 36 reads “… of those who went out to war, was in number three hundred thirty-seven thousand five hundred sheep:”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ spoke to the people, saying, Arm you men from among you for the war, that they may go against Midian, to execute Yahweh's vengeance on …” (31:3, WEB) Easy

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q6. A) possesses; cleave   B) brothers; cleave   C) possesses; Jericho   D) cleave; possesses
Answer: A. possesses; cleave. Verse 8 reads “Every daughter, who possesses an inheritance in any tribe of the children of Israel, shall be wife to one of the family of the tribe of …”; verse 9 reads “… to another tribe; for the tribes of the children of Israel shall cleave everyone to his own inheritance.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Deuteronomy
Deuteronomy 1
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… said, Let us send men before us, that they may search the wilderness for us, and bring us word again of the way by which we …” (1:22, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… said, Let us send men before us, that they may search the land for us, and bring us word again of the way by which we …” It says land, not wilderness.


Q2. True or false: Moses recounted Israel's history at the border of the promised land. (1:1-5) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 1:1 records: “These are the words which Moses spoke to all Israel beyond the Jordan in the wilderness, in the Arabah over against Suph, between Paran, and Tophel, and Laban, and Hazeroth, and Dizahab.”

Deuteronomy 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Your eyes have seen what Yahweh did because of Baal ______; for all the men who followed Baal Peor, Yahweh your God has destroyed them from …” (4:3, WEB) Easy

Q2. Multiple choice: Why does Moses connect the prohibition of images with Israel's experience at Horeb? (4:12, 15-19) Hard
A) The stars created Israel.   B) Israel received a statue to copy.   C) They heard God's voice but saw no form to reproduce.   D) God told Israel never to make any beautiful object.
Answer: C. They heard God's voice but saw no form to reproduce.. The argument uses the form of God's self-revelation as a reason against making an idol. It does not prohibit every kind of visual art.


Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, moses said, The LORD your God is a consuming ______. (4:24) Easy

Q4. Multiple choice: How are love for God and teaching children connected? (6:4-9) Hard
A) God's words are to shape the heart and the ordinary life of the household.   B) Children should learn only from foreign kings.   C) Remembering God matters only in the temple.   D) Outward symbols make attention to the heart unnecessary.
Answer: A. God's words are to shape the heart and the ordinary life of the household.. The commands move from love and the heart to conversation at home and on the road. Covenant faithfulness is learned in daily life, not confined to a ceremony.

Q7. Multiple choice: What reason does the text give for God's choosing and rescuing Israel? (7:6-9) Hard

Answer: B. forty. Deuteronomy 8:4 uses forty: “… clothing didn't grow old on you, neither did your foot swell, these forty years.”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “For Yahweh your God brings you into a good ______, a land of brooks of ______, of springs and springs, flowing forth in valleys and hills;” (8:7, WEB) Medium

Deuteronomy 10
Q1. Multiple choice: How does the command to love the resident foreigner reflect God's character? (10:17-19) Hard
A) God loves the foreigner and provides food and clothing.   B) God cares only for native-born Israelites.   C) Foreigners have no need of justice.   D) Israel must forget its experience in Egypt.
Answer: A. God loves the foreigner and provides food and clothing.. Israel is to reflect God's impartial justice and remember its own experience as foreigners in Egypt. This connects worship with treatment of vulnerable people.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “He does execute justice for the fatherless and ______, and loves the foreigner, in giving him food and clothing.” (10:18); “You shall ______ Yahweh your God; him shall you serve; and to him shall you cleave, and by his name shall you swear.” (10:20, WEB) Hard
Answer: widow; fear. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
