Deuteronomy 16
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… according to your tribes; and they shall judge the people with righteous ______.” (16:18); “You shall not wrest ______: you shall not respect persons; neither shall you take a bribe; for a bribe does blind the eyes of the wise, …” (16:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: judgment; justice. The first passage reads “… according to your tribes; and they shall judge the people with righteous judgment.”; the second reads “You shall not wrest justice: you shall not respect persons; neither shall you take a bribe; for a bribe does blind the eyes of the wise, …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the place which Yahweh your God shall ______, to cause his name to dwell in, there you shall ______ the Passover at even, at the going …” (16:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: choose; sacrifice. The wording is “… the place which Yahweh your God shall choose, to cause his name to dwell in, there you shall sacrifice the Passover at even, at the going …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Six days you shall eat unleavened ______; and on the seventh day shall be a solemn assembly to Yahweh your God; you shall do no work …”? (16:8, WEB) Medium
A) bread   B) olives   C) corn   D) figs
Answer: A. bread. The passage specifies bread: “Six days you shall eat unleavened bread; and on the seventh day shall be a solemn assembly to Yahweh your God; you shall do no work …”


Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “You shall ______ the Passover to Yahweh your God, of the flock and the herd, in the place which Yahweh shall choose, to cause his name …” (16:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: sacrifice. The cited words are “You shall sacrifice the Passover to Yahweh your God, of the flock and the herd, in the place which Yahweh shall choose, to cause his name …”


Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… is within your gates, and the foreigner, and the fatherless, and the widow, who are in the midst of you, in the place which Yahweh your …” (16:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… is within your gates, and the foreigner, and the fatherless, and the widow, who are in the midst of you, in the place which Yahweh your …”


Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “You shall keep the feast of tents ______ days, after that you have gathered in from your threshing floor and from your winepress:” (16:13); “… and in all the work of your hands, and you shall be ______ joyful.” (16:15, WEB) Hard
A) altogether; seven   B) seven; remember   C) fifth; altogether   D) seven; altogether
Answer: D. seven; altogether. Verse 13 reads “You shall keep the feast of tents seven days, after that you have gathered in from your threshing floor and from your winepress:”; verse 15 reads “… and in all the work of your hands, and you shall be altogether joyful.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q7. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, israel was to celebrate the Feast of ______ seven weeks after the harvest began. (16:9) Easy
Answer: Weeks. Deuteronomy 16:9 records: “Seven weeks shall you number to you: from the time you begin to put the sickle to the standing grain shall you begin to number seven weeks.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… seven days; neither shall any of the flesh, which you sacrifice the ______ day at even, remain all night until the morning.”? (16:4, WEB) Easy
A) ten   B) hundred   C) first   D) ninety
Answer: C. first. Deuteronomy 16:4 uses first: “… seven days; neither shall any of the flesh, which you sacrifice the first day at even, remain all night until the morning.”


Deuteronomy 17
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “according to the tenor of the testimony which they shall teach you, and according to the judgment which they shall tell you, you shall do; you …” (17:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “according to the tenor of the law which they shall teach you, and according to the judgment which they shall tell you, you shall do; you …” It says law, not testimony.


Q2. Multiple choice: How is Israel's king placed under the same covenant standards as the people? (17:14-20) Hard
A) He may replace God's law with his preferences.   B) The law applies to everyone except the king.   C) He must read the law and avoid exalting himself above his fellow Israelites.   D) Royal wealth proves that every decision is right.
Answer: C. He must read the law and avoid exalting himself above his fellow Israelites.. Restrictions on horses, wives and wealth accompany daily attention to the law. The course uses this passage as a standard for assessing Israel's kings.


Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “and has gone and served other gods, and ______ them, or the sun, or the moon, or any of the host of the sky, which I …” (17:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: worshiped. The cited words are “and has gone and served other gods, and worshiped them, or the sun, or the moon, or any of the host of the sky, which I …”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… arise a matter too hard for you in judgment, between blood and ______, between plea and plea, and between stroke and stroke, being matters of controversy …”? (17:8, WEB) Medium
A) tooth   B) blood   C) arm   D) mouth
Answer: B. blood. The passage specifies blood: “… arise a matter too hard for you in judgment, between blood and blood, between plea and plea, and between stroke and stroke, being matters of controversy …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… his heart not turn away: neither shall he greatly multiply to himself ______ and gold.” (17:17); “… this law in a book, out of that which is before the ______ the Levites:” (17:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: silver; priests. The first passage reads “… his heart not turn away: neither shall he greatly multiply to himself silver and gold.”; the second reads “… this law in a book, out of that which is before the priests the Levites:”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… and, behold, if it be true, and the thing certain, that such ______ is done in Israel,”? (17:4, WEB) Easy
A) mercy   B) promise   C) abomination   D) silver
Answer: C. abomination. Deuteronomy 17:4 uses abomination: “… and, behold, if it be true, and the thing certain, that such abomination is done in Israel,”


Q7. True or false: A king of Israel was not to multiply horses for himself. (17:16) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 17:16 records: “… he shall not multiply horses to himself, nor cause the people to return to Egypt, to the end that he may multiply horses; because Yahweh has said to you, You shall henceforth return no more that …”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… ______ witnesses, shall he who is to die be put to death; at the mouth of one witness he shall not be put to ______.” (17:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: three; death. The wording is “… three witnesses, shall he who is to die be put to death; at the mouth of one witness he shall not be put to death.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Deuteronomy 18
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For these ______, that you shall dispossess, listen to those who practice sorcery, and to diviners; but as for you, Yahweh your God has not allowed …” (18:14); “… desired of Yahweh your God in Horeb in the day of the ______, saying, Let me not hear again the voice of Yahweh my God, neither …” (18:16, WEB) Hard
A) nations; minister   B) assembly; nations   C) makes; assembly   D) nations; assembly
Answer: D. nations; assembly. Verse 14 reads “For these nations, that you shall dispossess, listen to those who practice sorcery, and to diviners; but as for you, Yahweh your God has not allowed …”; verse 16 reads “… desired of Yahweh your God in Horeb in the day of the assembly, saying, Let me not hear again the voice of Yahweh my God, neither …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, god would raise up a prophet like Moses from among their ______. (18:15) Easy
Answer: brothers. Deuteronomy 18:15 records: “Yahweh your God will raise up to you a prophet from the midst of you, of your brothers, like me; to him you shall listen;”


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “The first fruits of your ______, of your new wine, and of your oil, and the first of the fleece of your sheep, shall you give …”? (18:4, WEB) Easy
A) grain   B) flour   C) water   D) meat
Answer: A. grain. Deuteronomy 18:4 uses grain: “The first fruits of your grain, of your new wine, and of your oil, and the first of the fleece of your sheep, shall you give …”


Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “or a charmer, or a consulter with a familiar spirit, or a wizard, or a necromancer.” (18:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “or a charmer, or a consulter with a familiar spirit, or a wizard, or a necromancer.”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… shall have no portion nor inheritance with Israel: they shall eat the ______ of Yahweh made by fire, and his inheritance.” (18:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: offerings. The cited words are “… shall have no portion nor inheritance with Israel: they shall eat the offerings of Yahweh made by fire, and his inheritance.”


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… anyone who makes his son or his daughter to pass through the ______, one who uses divination, one who practices sorcery, or an enchanter, or a …”? (18:10, WEB) Medium
A) fire   B) pass   C) know   D) prophet
Answer: A. fire. The passage specifies fire: “… anyone who makes his son or his daughter to pass through the fire, one who uses divination, one who practices sorcery, or an enchanter, or a …”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “I will raise them up a prophet from among their ______, like you; and I will put my words in his mouth, and he shall speak …” (18:18); “But the prophet, who shall speak a word ______ in my name, which I have not commanded him to speak, or who shall speak in the …” (18:20, WEB) Hard
Answer: brothers; presumptuously. The first passage reads “I will raise them up a prophet from among their brothers, like you; and I will put my words in his mouth, and he shall speak …”; the second reads “But the prophet, who shall speak a word presumptuously in my name, which I have not commanded him to speak, or who shall speak in the …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “If a Levite comes from any of your ______ out of all ______, where he lives as a foreigner, and comes with all the desire of …” (18:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: gates; Israel. The wording is “If a Levite comes from any of your gates out of all Israel, where he lives as a foreigner, and comes with all the desire of …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Deuteronomy 19
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… is the case of the manslayer, that shall flee there and live: ______ kills his neighbor unawares, and didn't hate him in time past;” (19:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: whoever. The cited words are “… is the case of the manslayer, that shall flee there and live: whoever kills his neighbor unawares, and didn't hate him in time past;”


Q2. True or false: Cities of refuge were set up in the land. (19:1-3) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 19:1 records: “When Yahweh your God shall cut off the nations, whose land Yahweh your God gives you, and you succeed them, and dwell in their cities, and in their houses;”


Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the elders of his city shall send and bring him there, and ______ him into the hand of the avenger of blood, that he may die.” (19:12); “One witness shall not rise up against a man for any ______, or for any sin, in any sin that he sins: at the mouth of …” (19:15, WEB) Hard
A) deliver; iniquity   B) iniquity; deliver   C) deliver; idolatry   D) love; iniquity
Answer: A. deliver; iniquity. Verse 12 reads “… the elders of his city shall send and bring him there, and deliver him into the hand of the avenger of blood, that he may die.”; verse 15 reads “One witness shall not rise up against a man for any iniquity, or for any sin, in any sin that he sins: at the mouth of …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… axe to cut down the tree, and the head slips from the ______, and lights on his neighbor, so that he dies; he shall flee to …”? (19:5, WEB) Easy
A) head   B) land   C) handle   D) strike
Answer: C. handle. Deuteronomy 19:5 uses handle: “… axe to cut down the tree, and the head slips from the handle, and lights on his neighbor, so that he dies; he shall flee to …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “lest the avenger of ______ pursue the manslayer, while his ______ is hot, and overtake him, because the way is long, and strike him mortally; whereas …” (19:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: blood; heart. The wording is “lest the avenger of blood pursue the manslayer, while his heart is hot, and overtake him, because the way is long, and strike him mortally; whereas …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… this commandment to do it, which I command you this day, to ______ Yahweh your God, and to walk ever in his ways; then shall you …”? (19:9, WEB) Medium
A) justice   B) truth   C) grief   D) love
Answer: D. love. The passage specifies love: “… this commandment to do it, which I command you this day, to love Yahweh your God, and to walk ever in his ways; then shall you …”


Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… any man hate his neighbor, and lie in wait for him, and testified up against him, and strike him mortally so that he dies, and he …” (19:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… any man hate his neighbor, and lie in wait for him, and rise up against him, and strike him mortally so that he dies, and he …” It says rise, not testified.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… men, between whom the controversy is, shall stand before Yahweh, before the ______ and the judges who shall be in those days;” (19:17); “and the judges shall make diligent ______: and, behold, if the witness is a false witness, and has testified falsely against his brother;” (19:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: priests; inquisition. The first passage reads “… men, between whom the controversy is, shall stand before Yahweh, before the priests and the judges who shall be in those days;”; the second reads “and the judges shall make diligent inquisition: and, behold, if the witness is a false witness, and has testified falsely against his brother;”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Deuteronomy 20
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… you draw near to a city to fight against it, then proclaim peace to it.” (20:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… you draw near to a city to fight against it, then proclaim peace to it.”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “It shall be, if it make you ______ of peace, and open to you, then it shall be, that all the people who are found therein …” (20:11); “… the cattle, and all that is in the city, even all the ______ of it, shall you take for a prey to yourself; and you shall …” (20:14, WEB) Hard
A) answer; spoil   B) faint; spoil   C) spoil; answer   D) answer; captains
Answer: A. answer; spoil. Verse 11 reads “It shall be, if it make you answer of peace, and open to you, then it shall be, that all the people who are found therein …”; verse 14 reads “… the cattle, and all that is in the city, even all the spoil of it, shall you take for a prey to yourself; and you shall …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… dedicated it? let him go and return to his house, lest he ______ in the battle, and another man dedicate it.”? (20:5, WEB) Easy
A) fear   B) forgive   C) die   D) hate
Answer: C. die. Deuteronomy 20:5 uses die: “… dedicated it? let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the battle, and another man dedicate it.”


Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, when going to war, the priest was to tell them not to be ______. (20:3) Easy
Answer: afraid. Deuteronomy 20:3 records: “and shall tell them, Hear, Israel, you draw near this day to battle against your enemies: don't let your heart faint; don't be afraid, nor tremble, neither be scared of them;”


Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and return to his house, lest his brother's heart melt as his ______.”? (20:8, WEB) Medium
A) neck   B) heart   C) hair   D) eye
Answer: B. heart. The passage specifies heart: “… and return to his house, lest his brother's heart melt as his heart.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “What man is there who has ______ a ______, and has not used the fruit of it? let him go and return to his house, lest …” (20:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: planted; vineyard. The wording is “What man is there who has planted a vineyard, and has not used the fruit of it? let him go and return to his house, lest …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “It shall be, when you draw near to the battle, that the ______ shall approach and speak to the people,” (20:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: priest. The cited words are “It shall be, when you draw near to the battle, that the priest shall approach and speak to the people,”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… cities of these peoples, that Yahweh your God gives you for an ______, you shall save alive nothing that breathes;” (20:16); “… to take it, you shall not destroy the trees of it by ______ an axe against them; for you may eat of them, and you shall …” (20:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: inheritance; wielding. The first passage reads “… cities of these peoples, that Yahweh your God gives you for an inheritance, you shall save alive nothing that breathes;”; the second reads “… to take it, you shall not destroy the trees of it by wielding an axe against them; for you may eat of them, and you shall …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Deuteronomy 21
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “All the elders of that city, who are nearest to the ______ man, shall wash their hands over the heifer whose neck was broken in the …”? (21:6, WEB) Easy
A) Levi   B) slain   C) Forgive   D) firstborn
Answer: B. slain. Deuteronomy 21:6 uses slain: “All the elders of that city, who are nearest to the slain man, shall wash their hands over the heifer whose neck was broken in the …”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “If a man have ______ wives, the one beloved, and the other hated, and they have borne him children, both the beloved and the hated; and …” (21:15); “… it shall be, in the day that he causes his sons to ______ that which he has, that he may not make the son of the …” (21:16, WEB) Hard
A) two; inherit   B) six; inherit   C) inherit; two   D) two; heifer's
Answer: A. two; inherit. Verse 15 reads “If a man have two wives, the one beloved, and the other hated, and they have borne him children, both the beloved and the hated; and …”; verse 16 reads “… it shall be, in the day that he causes his sons to inherit that which he has, that he may not make the son of the …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The priests the sons of ______ shall come near; for them Yahweh your God has chosen to minister to him, and to bless in the name …” (21:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: Levi. The cited words are “The priests the sons of Levi shall come near; for them Yahweh your God has chosen to minister to him, and to bless in the name …”


Q4. True or false: A heifer was to be killed to atone for an unsolved murder. (21:1-4) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 21:1 records: “If one be found slain in the land which Yahweh your God gives you to possess it, lying in the field, and it isn't known who has struck him;”


Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… don't allow innocent blood to remain in the midst of your people ______. The blood shall be forgiven them.”? (21:8, WEB) Medium
A) Enoch   B) Zebulun   C) Rachel   D) Israel
Answer: D. Israel. The passage specifies Israel: “… don't allow innocent blood to remain in the midst of your people Israel. The blood shall be forgiven them.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “If a man have a stubborn and ______ son, who will not obey the voice of his father, or the voice of his mother, and, though …” (21:18); “All the men of his city shall stone him to ______ with stones: so shall you put away the evil from the midst of you; and …” (21:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: rebellious; death. The first passage reads “If a man have a stubborn and rebellious son, who will not obey the voice of his father, or the voice of his mother, and, though …”; the second reads “All the men of his city shall stone him to death with stones: so shall you put away the evil from the midst of you; and …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… in to her, and be her husband, and she shall be your priest.” (21:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… in to her, and be her husband, and she shall be your wife.” It says wife, not priest.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “and they shall answer and say, Our hands have not ______ this ______, neither have our eyes seen it.” (21:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: shed; blood. The wording is “and they shall answer and say, Our hands have not shed this blood, neither have our eyes seen it.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Deuteronomy 22
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “If your ______ isn't near to you, or if you don't know him, then you shall bring it home to your house, and it shall be …” (22:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: brother. The cited words are “If your brother isn't near to you, or if you don't know him, then you shall bring it home to your house, and it shall be …”


Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, a person was to return a lost ______ to its owner. (22:1) Easy
Answer: animal. Deuteronomy 22:1 records: “You shall not see your brother's ox or his sheep go astray, and hide yourself from them: you shall surely bring them again to your brother.”


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “A woman shall not wear ______ clothing, neither shall a man put on women's clothing; for whoever does these things is an abomination to Yahweh your …”? (22:5, WEB) Easy
A) eggs   B) men's   C) lady's   D) kills
Answer: B. men's. Deuteronomy 22:5 uses men's: “A woman shall not wear men's clothing, neither shall a man put on women's clothing; for whoever does these things is an abomination to Yahweh your …”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… things to her charge, saying, I didn't find in your daughter the ______ of virginity; and yet these are the tokens of my daughter's virginity. They …” (22:17); “… father's house, and the men of her city shall stone her to ______ with stones, because she has done folly in Israel, to play the prostitute …” (22:21, WEB) Hard
A) death; tokens   B) tokens; death   C) brother's; death   D) tokens; worship
Answer: B. tokens; death. Verse 17 reads “… things to her charge, saying, I didn't find in your daughter the tokens of virginity; and yet these are the tokens of my daughter's virginity. They …”; verse 21 reads “… father's house, and the men of her city shall stone her to death with stones, because she has done folly in Israel, to play the prostitute …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “then shall the father of the young lady, and her mother, take and bring forth the tokens of the young lady's virginity to the elders of …” (22:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “then shall the father of the young lady, and her mother, take and bring forth the tokens of the young lady's virginity to the elders of …”


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “You shall make you fringes on the ______ borders of your cloak, with which you cover yourself.”? (22:12, WEB) Medium
A) fourth   B) fourteen   C) fifth   D) four
Answer: D. four. The passage specifies four: “You shall make you fringes on the four borders of your cloak, with which you cover yourself.”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… your vineyard with ______ kinds of seed, lest the whole fruit be forfeited, the seed which you have sown, and the ______ of the vineyard.” (22:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: two; increase. The wording is “… your vineyard with two kinds of seed, lest the whole fruit be forfeited, the seed which you have sown, and the increase of the vineyard.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… being in the city; and the man, because he has humbled his ______ wife: so you shall put away the evil from the midst of you.” (22:24); “… the lady you shall do nothing; there is in the lady no ______ worthy of death: for as when a man rises against his neighbor, and …” (22:26, WEB) Hard
Answer: neighbor's; sin. The first passage reads “… being in the city; and the man, because he has humbled his neighbor's wife: so you shall put away the evil from the midst of you.”; the second reads “… the lady you shall do nothing; there is in the lady no sin worthy of death: for as when a man rises against his neighbor, and …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Deuteronomy 23
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… go abroad out of the camp, he shall not come within the ______:”? (23:10, WEB) Medium
A) choose   B) camp   C) place   D) peace
Answer: B. camp. The passage specifies camp: “… go abroad out of the camp, he shall not come within the camp:”


Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “You shall not abhor an ______; for he is your brother: you shall not ______ an Egyptian, because you lived as a foreigner in his land.” (23:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Edomite; abhor. The wording is “You shall not abhor an Edomite; for he is your brother: you shall not abhor an Egyptian, because you lived as a foreigner in his land.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q3. True or false: No Ammonite or Moabite could enter the assembly of the LORD. (23:3) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 23:3 records: “An Ammonite or a Moabite shall not enter into the assembly of Yahweh; even to the tenth generation shall none belonging to them enter into the assembly of Yahweh forever:”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “because they didn't meet you with ______ and with water in the way, when you came forth out of Egypt, and because they hired against you …”? (23:4, WEB) Easy
A) bread   B) grapes   C) pomegranates   D) wheat
Answer: A. bread. Deuteronomy 23:4 uses bread: “because they didn't meet you with bread and with water in the way, when you came forth out of Egypt, and because they hired against you …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… you may lend on interest; but to your brother you shall not ______ on interest, that Yahweh your God may bless you in all that you …” (23:20); “… and do; according as you have vowed to Yahweh your God, a freewill-______, which you have promised with your mouth.” (23:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: lend; offering. The first passage reads “… you may lend on interest; but to your brother you shall not lend on interest, that Yahweh your God may bless you in all that you …”; the second reads “… and do; according as you have vowed to Yahweh your God, a freewill-offering, which you have promised with your mouth.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… weapons; and it shall be, when you sit down abroad, you shall enemies therewith, and shall turn back and cover that which comes from you:” (23:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… weapons; and it shall be, when you sit down abroad, you shall dig therewith, and shall turn back and cover that which comes from you:” It says dig, not enemies.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… to give up your enemies before you; therefore shall your camp be ______, that he may not see an unclean thing in you, and turn away …” (23:14); “… of Israel, neither shall there be a sodomite of the sons of ______.” (23:17, WEB) Hard
A) Israel; holy   B) holy; Israel   C) holy; Balak   D) rich; Israel
Answer: B. holy; Israel. Verse 14 reads “… to give up your enemies before you; therefore shall your camp be holy, that he may not see an unclean thing in you, and turn away …”; verse 17 reads “… of Israel, neither shall there be a sodomite of the sons of Israel.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… bastard shall not enter into the assembly of Yahweh; even to the ______ generation shall none of his enter into the assembly of Yahweh.” (23:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: tenth. The cited words are “… bastard shall not enter into the assembly of Yahweh; even to the tenth generation shall none of his enter into the assembly of Yahweh.”


Deuteronomy 24
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… leprosy, that you observe diligently, and do according to all that the ______ the Levites shall teach you: as I commanded them, so you shall observe …”? (24:8, WEB) Medium
A) shepherd   B) fathers   C) rulers   D) priests
Answer: D. priests. The passage specifies priests: “… leprosy, that you observe diligently, and do according to all that the priests the Levites shall teach you: as I commanded them, so you shall observe …”


Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… of his brothers of the children of Israel, and he ______ with him as a ______, or sell him; then that thief shall die: so shall …” (24:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: deal; slave. The wording is “… of his brothers of the children of Israel, and he deal with him as a slave, or sell him; then that thief shall die: so shall …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… is abomination before Yahweh: and you shall not cause the land to ______, which Yahweh your God gives you for an inheritance.”? (24:4, WEB) Easy
A) disobedience   B) sin   C) rebellion   D) violence
Answer: B. sin. Deuteronomy 24:4 uses sin: “… is abomination before Yahweh: and you shall not cause the land to sin, which Yahweh your God gives you for an inheritance.”


Q4. True or false: The divorce certificate discussed here is a command that husbands must divorce their wives. (24:1-4) Hard
Answer: False. The law regulates a case involving divorce and remarriage; it does not command divorce. Jesus later discusses Moses' provision in relation to human hardness of heart in Matthew 19:3-9.


Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “You shall not oppress a hired servant who is ______ and needy, whether he be of your brothers, or of your foreigners who are in your …” (24:14); “… put to death for the fathers: every man shall be put to ______ for his own sin.” (24:16, WEB) Hard
A) poor; power   B) poor; death   C) gracious; death   D) death; poor
Answer: B. poor; death. Verse 14 reads “You shall not oppress a hired servant who is poor and needy, whether he be of your brothers, or of your foreigners who are in your …”; verse 16 reads “… put to death for the fathers: every man shall be put to death for his own sin.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… or if the latter husband die, who took her to be his ______;” (24:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: wife. The cited words are “… or if the latter husband die, who took her to be his wife;”


Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “You shall inheritance outside, and the man to whom you do lend shall bring forth the pledge outside to you.” (24:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “You shall stand outside, and the man to whom you do lend shall bring forth the pledge outside to you.” It says stand, not inheritance.


Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, a man who had recently married was exempt from ______ for one year. (24:5) Easy
Answer: war. Deuteronomy 24:5 records: “… man takes a new wife, he shall not go out in the host, neither shall he be charged with any business: he shall be free at home one year, and shall cheer his wife whom he …”

Q2. True or false: The ox treading grain was not to be muzzled. (25:4) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 25:4 records: “You shall not muzzle the ox when he treads out the grain.”

Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… feeble behind you, when you were faint and weary; and he didn't ______ God.” (25:18); “… about, in the land which Yahweh your God gives you for an ______ to possess it, that you shall blot out the memory of Amalek from …” (25:19, WEB) Hard

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q7. Answer: D. deliver; land. Verse 11 reads “… one with another, and the wife of the one draws near to deliver her husband out of the hand of him who strikes him, and puts …”; verse 15 reads “… measure shall you have: that your days may be long in the land which Yahweh your God gives you.”. Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Cursed be he who lies with his father's ______, because he has uncovered his father's skirt. All the people shall say, Amen.” (27:20); “Cursed be he who lies with his ______, the daughter of his father, or the daughter of his mother. All the people shall say, Amen.” (27:22, WEB) Hard
Answer: wife; sister. The first passage reads “Cursed be he who lies with his father's wife, because he has uncovered his father's skirt. All the people shall say, Amen.”; the second reads “Cursed be he who lies with his sister, the daughter of his father, or the daughter of his mother. All the people shall say, Amen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Deuteronomy 28
Q1. Multiple choice: Why should these blessings and curses not be treated as an automatic prediction of every individual's health or wealth? (28:1-2, 15, 36, 64) Hard
A) Every poor person is being individually cursed.   B) The chapter contains no warnings.   C) The passage guarantees riches to every reader.   D) They address Israel's covenant life in the land, including national exile.
Answer: D. They address Israel's covenant life in the land, including national exile.. Their setting is a covenant with Israel. The book of Job and many psalms also resist the simplistic conclusion that every sufferer must be unusually wicked.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… down, in which you trusted, throughout all your land; and they shall ______ you in all your gates throughout all your land, which Yahweh your God …” (28:52); “then Yahweh will make your ______ wonderful, and the plagues of your seed, even great plagues, and of long continuance, and sore sicknesses, and of long …” (28:59, WEB) Hard
Answer: besiege; plagues. The first passage reads “… down, in which you trusted, throughout all your land; and they shall besiege you in all your gates throughout all your land, which Yahweh your God …”; the second reads “then Yahweh will make your plagues wonderful, and the plagues of your seed, even great plagues, and of long continuance, and sore sicknesses, and of long …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… of your cattle, and in the fruit of your ground, in the ______ which Yahweh swore to your fathers to give you.”? (28:11, WEB) Easy
A) valley   B) vineyards   C) land   D) countries
Answer: C. land. Deuteronomy 28:11 uses land: “… of your cattle, and in the fruit of your ground, in the land which Yahweh swore to your fathers to give you.”


Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… shall be given to your enemies, and you shall have none to keep you.” (28:31, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… shall be given to your enemies, and you shall have none to save you.” It says save, not keep.


Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the blessings for obedience included being the head and not the ______. (28:13) Easy
Answer: tail. Deuteronomy 28:13 records: “… the head, and not the tail; and you shall be above only, and you shall not be beneath; if you shall listen to the commandments of Yahweh your God, which I command you this day, to …”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Cursed shall be the fruit of your ______, and the fruit of your ground, the increase of your ______, and the young of your flock.” (28:18, WEB) Medium
Answer: body; cattle. The wording is “Cursed shall be the fruit of your body, and the fruit of your ground, the increase of your cattle, and the young of your flock.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… enemies; you shall go out one way against them, and shall flee ______ ways before them: and you shall be tossed back and forth among all …”? (28:25, WEB) Medium
A) nineteen   B) ninety   C) third   D) seven
Answer: D. seven. The passage specifies seven: “… enemies; you shall go out one way against them, and shall flee seven ways before them: and you shall be tossed back and forth among all …”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… shall be the fruit of your body, and the fruit of your ______, and the fruit of your animals, the increase of your cattle, and the …” (28:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: ground. The cited words are “… shall be the fruit of your body, and the fruit of your ground, and the fruit of your animals, the increase of your cattle, and the …”

Deuteronomy 30
Q1. Multiple choice: What holds together God's promised renewal and Israel's call to choose life? (30:6, 11-20) Hard
A) God's help is never mentioned.   B) Human response is never mentioned.   C) Choosing life means rejecting God's voice.   D) God promises to work in their hearts while calling them to love and obey Him.
Answer: D. God promises to work in their hearts while calling them to love and obey Him.. The chapter includes both divine action and meaningful human response. It should not be used to pretend that every later debate about grace and free will is settled by one isolated verse.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… your God will circumcise your heart, and the heart of your ______, to ______ Yahweh your God with all your heart, and with all your soul, …” (30:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: seed; love. The wording is “… your God will circumcise your heart, and the heart of your seed, to love Yahweh your God with all your heart, and with all your soul, …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… in all the work of your hand, in the fruit of your ______, and in the fruit of your cattle, and in the fruit of your …”? (30:9, WEB) Medium
A) hate   B) commandments   C) body   D) ordinances
Answer: C. body. The passage specifies body: “… in all the work of your hand, in the fruit of your body, and in the fruit of your cattle, and in the fruit of your …”


Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… the voice of Yahweh your God, to keep his commandments and his statutes which are written in this book of the law; if you turn to …” (30:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… the voice of Yahweh your God, to keep his commandments and his statutes which are written in this book of the law; if you turn to …”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… I command you this day, you and your children, with all your ______, and with all your soul;” (30:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: heart. The cited words are “… I command you this day, you and your children, with all your heart, and with all your soul;”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… you will not hear, but shall be drawn away, and worship other ______, and serve them;” (30:17); “… not prolong your days in the land, where you pass over the ______ to go in to possess it.” (30:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: gods; Jordan. The first passage reads “… you will not hear, but shall be drawn away, and worship other gods, and serve them;”; the second reads “… not prolong your days in the land, where you pass over the Jordan to go in to possess it.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q7. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, moses said the commandment is not too hard, nor is it too ______. (30:11) Easy
Answer: far. Deuteronomy 30:11 records: “For this commandment which I command you this day, it is not too hard for you, neither is it far off.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… compassion on you, and will return and gather you from all the ______, where Yahweh your God has scattered you.”? (30:3, WEB) Easy
A) fathers   B) statutes   C) parts   D) peoples
Answer: D. peoples. Deuteronomy 30:3 uses peoples: “… compassion on you, and will return and gather you from all the peoples, where Yahweh your God has scattered you.”

The first passage reads “… good courage; for you shall bring the children of Israel into the land which I swore to them: and I will be with you.”; the second reads “… law, and put it by the side of the ark of the covenant of Yahweh your God, that it may be there for a witness against …”. Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Then my anger shall be ______ against them in that day, and I will forsake them, and I will hide my face from them, and they …” (31:17); “… this day, before I have brought them into the land which I ______.” (31:21, WEB) Hard
A) swore; kindled   B) happened; swore   C) kindled; swore   D) kindled; assembly
Answer: C. kindled; swore. Verse 17 reads “Then my anger shall be kindled against them in that day, and I will forsake them, and I will hide my face from them, and they …”; verse 21 reads “… this day, before I have brought them into the land which I swore.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… and Yahweh has said to me, You shall not go over this ______.” (31:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jordan. The cited words are “… and Yahweh has said to me, You shall not go over this Jordan.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… Yahweh your God in the place which he shall choose, you shall ______ this law before all Israel in their hearing.”? (31:11, WEB) Medium
A) prostitute   B) read   C) ones   D) sons
Answer: B. read. The passage specifies read: “… Yahweh your God in the place which he shall choose, you shall read this law before all Israel in their hearing.”


Deuteronomy 32
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… doctrine shall drop as the rain; My speech shall condense as the ______, As the small rain on the tender grass, As the showers on the …” (32:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: dew. The cited words are “… doctrine shall drop as the rain; My speech shall condense as the dew, As the small rain on the tender grass, As the showers on the …”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “When the Most High gave to the nations their ______, When he separated the children of men, He set the bounds of the peoples According to …”? (32:8, WEB) Easy
A) redemption   B) praise   C) strength   D) inheritance
Answer: D. inheritance. Deuteronomy 32:8 uses inheritance: “When the Most High gave to the nations their inheritance, When he separated the children of men, He set the bounds of the peoples According to …”


Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “If I whet my glittering sword, My hand take hold on ______; I will render vengeance to my adversaries, Will recompense those who hate me.” (32:41); “… your children to observe to do, even all the words of this ______.” (32:46, WEB) Hard
Answer: judgment; law. The first passage reads “If I whet my glittering sword, My hand take hold on judgment; I will render vengeance to my adversaries, Will recompense those who hate me.”; the second reads “… your children to observe to do, even all the words of this law.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “How should one chase a thousand, Two put ______ thousand to flight, Except their Rock had sold them, Yahweh had delivered them up?” (32:30); “… at hand, The things that are to come on them shall make ______.” (32:35, WEB) Hard
A) ten; haste   B) haste; ten   C) five; haste   D) ten; separated
Answer: A. ten; haste. Verse 30 reads “How should one chase a thousand, Two put ten thousand to flight, Except their Rock had sold them, Yahweh had delivered them up?”; verse 35 reads “… at hand, The things that are to come on them shall make haste.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the Song of Moses calls God a Rock whose work is ______. (32:4) Easy
Answer: perfect. Deuteronomy 32:4 records: “The Rock, his work is perfect; For all his ways are justice: A God of faithfulness and without iniquity, Just and right is he.”


Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… not a people; I will provoke them to anger with a foolish ______.”? (32:21, WEB) Medium
A) provocation   B) destruction   C) protection   D) nation
Answer: D. nation. The passage specifies nation: “… not a people; I will provoke them to anger with a foolish nation.”


Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… Bashan, and goats, With the finest of the ______; Of the blood of the grape you drank ______.” (32:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: wheat; wine. The wording is “… Bashan, and goats, With the finest of the wheat; Of the blood of the grape you drank wine.” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… teeth of animals will I send on them, With the poison of crawling things of the dust.” (32:24, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… teeth of animals will I send on them, With the poison of crawling things of the dust.”


Deuteronomy 33
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “He said, Yahweh came from ______, Rose from Seir to them; He shined forth from Mount Paran, He came from the ten thousands of holy ones: …” (33:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Sinai. The cited words are “He said, Yahweh came from Sinai, Rose from Seir to them; He shined forth from Mount Paran, He came from the ten thousands of holy ones: …”


Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… They are the ten thousands of Ephraim, They are the thousands of ______.” (33:17); “Of ______ he said, Blessed be he who enlarges Gad: He dwells as a lioness, Tears the arm, yes, the crown of the head.” (33:20, WEB) Hard
A) Gad; Manasseh   B) Manasseh; Gad   C) Manasseh; Enoch   D) Saul; Gad
Answer: B. Manasseh; Gad. Verse 17 reads “… They are the ten thousands of Ephraim, They are the thousands of Manasseh.”; verse 20 reads “Of Gad he said, Blessed be he who enlarges Gad: He dwells as a lioness, Tears the arm, yes, the crown of the head.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.


Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He was ______ in Jeshurun, When the heads of the people were gathered, All the tribes of Israel together.”? (33:5, WEB) Easy
A) king   B) judge   C) fathers   D) soldier
Answer: A. king. Deuteronomy 33:5 uses king: “He was king in Jeshurun, When the heads of the people were gathered, All the tribes of Israel together.”


Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… rise up against him, Of those who hate him, that they not ______ again.”? (33:11, WEB) Medium
A) push   B) rise   C) covenant   D) bush
Answer: B. rise. The passage specifies rise: “… rise up against him, Of those who hate him, that they not rise again.”


Q5. True or false: Moses blessed the tribes of Israel before his death. (33:1) Easy
Answer: True. Deuteronomy 33:1 records: “This is the blessing, with which Moses the man of God blessed the children of Israel before his death.”


Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Of ______ he said, Your Thummim and your Urim are with your godly one, Whom you did ______ at Massah, With whom you did strive at …” (33:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: Levi; prove. The wording is “Of Levi he said, Your Thummim and your Urim are with your godly one, Whom you did prove at Massah, With whom you did strive at …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Of Joseph he said, Blessed of Yahweh be his land, For the precious things of the heavens, for the dew, For the deep that couches beneath,” (33:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Of Joseph he said, Blessed of Yahweh be his land, For the precious things of the heavens, for the dew, For the deep that couches beneath,”


Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Of Naphtali he said, ______, satisfied with favor, Full with the blessing of Yahweh, Possess you the west and the south.” (33:23); “… is none like God, Jeshurun, Who rides on the heavens for your ______, In his excellency on the skies.” (33:26, WEB) Hard
Answer: Naphtali; help. The first passage reads “Of Naphtali he said, Naphtali, satisfied with favor, Full with the blessing of Yahweh, Possess you the west and the south.”; the second reads “… is none like God, Jeshurun, Who rides on the heavens for your help, In his excellency on the skies.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Deuteronomy 34
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “There has not arisen a prophet since in Israel like ______, whom Yahweh knew face to face,” (34:10); “… the wonders, which Yahweh sent him to do in the land of ______, to Pharaoh, and to all his servants, and to all his land,” (34:11, WEB) Hard
Answer: Moses; Egypt. The first passage reads “There has not arisen a prophet since in Israel like Moses, whom Yahweh knew face to face,”; the second reads “… the wonders, which Yahweh sent him to do in the land of Egypt, to Pharaoh, and to all his servants, and to all his land,”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.


Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “and all ______, and the land of Ephraim and Manasseh, and all the land of Judah, to the hinder sea,” (34:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Naphtali. The cited words are “and all Naphtali, and the land of Ephraim and Manasseh, and all the land of Judah, to the hinder sea,”


Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Moses was one hundred ten years old when he died: his eye was not dim, nor his natural force abated.” (34:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Moses was one hundred twenty years old when he died: his eye was not dim, nor his natural force abated.” It says twenty, not ten.


Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and the South, and the Plain of the valley of ______ the city of palm trees, to Zoar.”? (34:3, WEB) Easy
A) Babylon   B) Jericho   C) Moab   D) Canaan
Answer: B. Jericho. Deuteronomy 34:3 uses Jericho: “and the South, and the Plain of the valley of Jericho the city of palm trees, to Zoar.”


Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… him, This is the land which I swore to Abraham, to ______, and to ______, saying, I will give it to your seed: I have caused …” (34:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: Isaac; Jacob. The wording is “… him, This is the land which I swore to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob, saying, I will give it to your seed: I have caused …” The missing terms belong in that order.


Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, moses died on Mount Nebo, and no one knows his ______ to this day. (34:6) Easy
Answer: grave. Deuteronomy 34:6 records: “He buried him in the valley in the land of Moab over against Beth Peor: but no man knows of his tomb to this day.”


Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “So Moses the ______ of Yahweh died there in the land of Moab, according to the word of Yahweh.”? (34:5, WEB) Medium
A) king   B) judge   C) ruler   D) servant
Answer: D. servant. The passage specifies servant: “So Moses the servant of Yahweh died there in the land of Moab, according to the word of Yahweh.”


Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The children of Israel wept for Moses in the plains of ______ thirty days: so the days of weeping in the mourning for Moses were ended.” (34:8); “Joshua the son of Nun was full of the spirit of ______; for Moses had laid his hands on him: and the children of Israel listened …” (34:9, WEB) Hard
A) Moab; grief   B) Zion; wisdom   C) wisdom; Moab   D) Moab; wisdom
Answer: D. Moab; wisdom. Verse 8 reads “The children of Israel wept for Moses in the plains of Moab thirty days: so the days of weeping in the mourning for Moses were ended.”; verse 9 reads “Joshua the son of Nun was full of the spirit of wisdom; for Moses had laid his hands on him: and the children of Israel listened …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
